    25→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    26→        return

    32→

    36→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    37→        return
    38→

   172→        return

   174→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, finding_ids)

   181→

   190→

   198→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   199→    """Reopen resolved findings from plan context."""
   200→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   201→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   202→
   203→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   204→
   205→    state_file = state_path(args)
   206→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   207→
   208→    reopened: list[str] = []
   209→    for pattern in patterns:
   210→        reopened.extend(
   211→            state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, pattern, "open")
   212→        )
   213→
   214→    if not reopened:
   215→        print(colorize("  No resolved findings matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   216→        return
   217→
   218→    state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   219→
   220→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   221→    plan = load_plan()
   222→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   223→    count = 0
   224→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   225→    for fid in reopened:
   226→        if fid in skipped:
   227→            skipped.pop(fid)
   228→            count += 1
   229→        if fid not in order:
   230→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   231→            order.add(fid)
   232→            count += 1
   233→    if count:
   234→        save_plan(plan)
   235→
   236→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s).", "green"))
   237→    if count:
   238→        print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))

    40→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    41→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    42→    try:
    43→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    44→    except OSError as exc:
    45→        print_error(f"could not save state: {exc}")
    46→        sys.exit(1)
    47→

    49→def _save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    50→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    51→    try:
    52→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    53→    except OSError as exc:
    54→        print_error(f"could not save config: {exc}")
    55→        sys.exit(1)

    58→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    59→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    60→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    61→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    62→
    63→    state_file = state_path(args)
    64→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    65→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    66→        state,
    67→        args,
    68→        attestation=attestation,
    69→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    70→    )
    71→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    72→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    73→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    74→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    75→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
    79→    if not all_resolved:
    80→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    81→        print(
    82→            colorize(
    83→                f"No {status_label} findings matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}",
    84→                "yellow",
    85→            )
    86→        )
    87→        return
    88→
    89→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    90→
    91→    # Remove resolved items from the living plan queue (best-effort)
    92→    try:
    93→        from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan, purge_ids, save_plan
    94→
    95→        if has_living_plan():
    96→            plan = load_plan()
    97→            purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    98→            if purged:
    99→                save_plan(plan)
   100→                print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
   101→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):

   105→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(

   109→    )
   110→    has_review_findings = any(

   112→        for fid in all_resolved
   113→    )
   114→    _print_score_movement(

   117→        prev_objective=prev.objective,

   126→        all_resolved=all_resolved,

   128→    )
   129→
   130→    lang = resolve_lang(args)

   136→    if narrative.get("milestone"):

   157→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   158→    """Add a pattern to the ignore list."""
   159→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   160→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   161→        show_attestation_requirement("Ignore", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   162→        sys.exit(1)
   163→
   164→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   165→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   166→    state = runtime.state
   167→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   168→
   169→    config = runtime.config
   170→    config_mod.add_ignore_pattern(config, args.pattern)
   171→    config["needs_rescan"] = True
   172→    _save_config_or_exit(config)
   173→
   174→    removed = state_mod.remove_ignored_findings(state, args.pattern)
   175→    state.setdefault("attestation_log", []).append(
   176→        {
   177→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
   178→            "command": "ignore",
   179→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   180→            "attestation": attestation,
   181→            "affected": removed,
   182→        }
   183→    )
   184→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   185→
   186→    print(colorize(f"Added ignore pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
   187→    if removed:
   188→        print(f"  Removed {removed} matching findings from state.")
   189→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   190→    if config_warning:
   191→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   192→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   193→
   194→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   195→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   196→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   197→        state,
   198→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="ignore"),
   199→    )
   200→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   201→    write_query(
   202→        {
   203→            "command": "ignore",
   204→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   205→            "removed": removed,

   209→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,

1→"""State-application helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query_best_effort as _write_query_best_effort
     9→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    10→
    11→from .selection import ResolveQueryContext
    12→
    13→
    14→def _try_expand_cluster(pattern: str) -> list[str] | None:
    15→    """If pattern matches a cluster name, return its finding IDs."""
    16→    try:
    17→        from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
    18→
    19→        if not has_living_plan():
    20→            return None
    21→        plan = load_plan()
    22→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(pattern)
    23→        if cluster and cluster.get("finding_ids"):
    24→            return list(cluster["finding_ids"])
    25→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    26→        pass
    27→    return None
    28→
    29→
    30→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    33→    *,
    34→    attestation: str | None,
    35→) -> list[str]:
    36→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    37→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    38→        # Check if pattern is a cluster name — expand to member IDs
    39→        cluster_ids = _try_expand_cluster(pattern)
    40→        if cluster_ids:
    41→            for fid in cluster_ids:
    42→                resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    43→                    state,
    44→                    fid,
    45→                    args.status,
    46→                    args.note,
    47→                    attestation=attestation,
    48→                )
    49→                all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    50→            continue
    51→
    52→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    53→            state,
    54→            pattern,
    55→            args.status,
    56→            args.note,
    57→            attestation=attestation,
    58→        )
    59→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    60→    return all_resolved
    61→
    62→
    63→def write_query(payload: dict) -> OutputResult:
    64→    """Backward-compatible resolve query writer seam."""
    65→    return _write_query_best_effort(
    66→        payload,
    67→        context="resolve query payload update",
    68→    )
    69→
    70→
    71→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    72→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)
    73→    write_query(
    74→        {
    75→            "command": "resolve",
    76→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    77→            "status": context.status,
    78→            "resolved": context.resolved,
    79→            "count": len(context.resolved),
    80→            "next_command": context.next_command,
    81→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    82→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    83→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    84→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    85→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,
    86→            "prev_objective_score": context.prev_objective,
    87→            "prev_strict_score": context.prev_strict,
    88→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    89→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    90→            "narrative": context.narrative,
    91→        }
    92→    )
    93→

1→"""Validation and preflight helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import copy
     7→import sys
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
    15→_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT = ("I have actually", "not gaming")
    16→
    17→
    18→def _emit_warning(message: str) -> None:
    19→    """Write resolve preflight warnings to stderr consistently."""
    20→    print(colorize(message, "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class ResolveQueryContext:
    25→    patterns: list[str]
    26→    status: str
    27→    resolved: list[str]
    28→    next_command: str
    29→    prev_overall: float | None
    30→    prev_objective: float | None
    31→    prev_strict: float | None
    32→    prev_verified: float | None
    33→    attestation: str | None
    34→    narrative: dict
    35→    state: dict
    36→
    37→
    38→def _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation: str | None) -> list[str]:
    39→    normalized = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
    40→    return [
    41→        phrase for phrase in _REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES if phrase not in normalized
    42→    ]
    43→
    44→
    45→def validate_attestation(attestation: str | None) -> bool:
    46→    return not _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    47→
    48→
    49→def show_attestation_requirement(
    50→    label: str,
    51→    attestation: str | None,
    52→    example: str,
    53→    *,
    54→    patterns: list[str] | None = None,
    55→    status: str | None = None,
    56→) -> None:
    57→    missing = _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    58→    if not attestation:
    59→        _emit_warning(f"{label} requires --attest.")
    60→    elif missing:
    61→        missing_str = ", ".join(f"'{keyword}'" for keyword in missing)
    62→        _emit_warning(
    63→            f"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."
    64→        )
    65→    _emit_warning(
    66→        f"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."
    67→    )
    68→    print(colorize(f'Example: --attest "{example}"', "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    69→    if patterns and status:
    70→        pattern_args = " ".join(f'"{p}"' for p in patterns)
    71→        print(
    72→            colorize(
    73→                f'\nCopy-paste:\n  desloppify resolve {status} {pattern_args} --attest "{example}"',
    74→                "dim",
    75→            ),
    76→            file=sys.stderr,
    77→        )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _validate_resolve_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    81→    if args.status == "wontfix" and not args.note:
    82→        _emit_warning(
    83→            "Wontfix items become technical debt. Add --note to record your reasoning for future review."
    84→        )
    85→        sys.exit(1)
    86→    if args.status == "open":
    87→        return
    88→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
    89→        show_attestation_requirement(
    90→            "Manual resolve",
    91→            attestation,
    92→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    93→            patterns=getattr(args, "patterns", None),
    94→            status=args.status,
    95→        )
    96→        sys.exit(1)
    97→
    98→
    99→def _previous_score_snapshot(state: dict) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:
   100→    """Load a score snapshot for comparison after resolve operations."""
   101→    return state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   102→
   103→
   104→def _preview_resolve_count(state: dict, patterns: list[str]) -> int:
   105→    """Count unique open findings matching the provided patterns."""
   106→    matched_ids: set[str] = set()
   107→    for pattern in patterns:
   108→        for finding in state_mod.match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter="open"):
   109→            finding_id = finding.get("id")
   110→            if finding_id:
   111→                matched_ids.add(finding_id)
   112→    return len(matched_ids)
   113→
   114→
   115→def _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   116→    state: dict,
   117→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   118→    *,
   119→    attestation: str | None,
   120→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   121→) -> float:
   122→    """Estimate strict score drop if this resolve command is applied as wontfix."""
   123→    before = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   124→    if before is None:
   125→        return 0.0
   126→
   127→    preview_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   128→    resolve_all_patterns_fn(preview_state, args, attestation=attestation)
   129→    after = state_mod.get_strict_score(preview_state)
   130→    if after is None:
   131→        return 0.0
   132→    return max(0.0, before - after)
   133→
   134→
   135→def _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   136→    state: dict,
   137→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   138→    *,
   139→    attestation: str | None,
   140→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   141→) -> None:
   142→    if args.status != "wontfix":
   143→        return
   144→
   145→    preview_count = _preview_resolve_count(state, args.patterns)
   146→    if preview_count <= 10:
   147→        return
   148→    if getattr(args, "confirm_batch_wontfix", False):
   149→        return
   150→
   151→    strict_delta = _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   152→        state,
   153→        args,
   154→        attestation=attestation,
   155→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   156→    )
   157→    _emit_warning(f"Large wontfix batch detected ({preview_count} findings).")
   158→    if strict_delta > 0:
   159→        _emit_warning(f"Estimated strict-score debt added now: {strict_delta:.1f} points.")
   160→    _emit_warning(
   161→        "Re-run with --confirm-batch-wontfix if this debt is intentional."
   162→    )
   163→    sys.exit(1)
   164→

    10→
    11→class _DeprecatedAction(argparse.Action):
    12→    """Argparse action that prints a deprecation warning and stores the value."""
    13→
    14→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    15→        print(
    16→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    17→            file=sys.stderr,
    18→        )
    19→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, values)
    20→
    21→
    22→class _DeprecatedBoolAction(argparse.Action):
    23→    """Argparse action for deprecated boolean flags (store_true equivalent)."""
    24→
    25→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    26→        kwargs.setdefault("nargs", 0)
    27→        kwargs.setdefault("const", True)
    28→        kwargs.setdefault("default", False)
    29→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    30→
    31→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    32→        print(
    33→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    34→            file=sys.stderr,
    35→        )
    36→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, True)

    41→        "detect",
    42→        help="Run a single detector directly (bypass state)",
    43→        epilog=f"detectors: {', '.join(detector_names)}",
    44→    )
    45→    p_detect.add_argument("detector", type=str, help="Detector to run")
    46→    p_detect.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=20, help="Max items to show (default: 20)")
    47→    p_detect.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
    48→    p_detect.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Output as JSON")
    49→    p_detect.add_argument(
    50→        "--fix",
    51→        action="store_true",
    52→        help="Auto-fix detected issues (logs detector only)",
    53→    )
    54→    p_detect.add_argument(
    55→        "--category",
    56→        choices=["imports", "vars", "params", "all"],
    57→        default="all",
    58→        help="Filter unused by category",
    59→    )
    60→    p_detect.add_argument(
    61→        "--threshold",
    62→        type=float,
    63→        default=None,
    64→        help="LOC threshold (large) or similarity (dupes)",
    65→    )
    66→    p_detect.add_argument(
    67→        "--file", type=str, default=None, help="Show deps for specific file"
    68→    )
    69→    p_detect.add_argument(
    70→        "--lang-opt",
    71→        action="append",
    72→        default=None,
    73→        metavar="KEY=VALUE",
    74→        help="Language runtime option override (repeatable)",
    75→    )

    91→def _add_review_parser(sub) -> None:
    92→    p_review = sub.add_parser(
    93→        "review",
    94→        help="Prepare or import holistic subjective review",
    95→        description="Run holistic subjective reviews using LLM-based analysis.",
    96→        epilog="""\
    97→examples:
    98→  desloppify review --prepare
    99→  desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
   100→  desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude
   101→  desloppify review --external-submit --session-id <id> --import findings.json
   102→  desloppify review --merge --similarity 0.8""",
   103→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   104→    )
   105→
   106→    # -- core options --
   107→    g_core = p_review.add_argument_group("core options")
   108→    g_core.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   109→    g_core.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   110→    g_core.add_argument(
   111→        "--prepare",
   112→        action="store_true",
   113→        help="Prepare review data (output to query.json)",
   114→    )
   115→    g_core.add_argument(
   116→        "--import",
   117→        dest="import_file",
   118→        type=str,
   119→        metavar="FILE",
   120→        help="Import review findings from JSON file",
   121→    )
   122→    g_core.add_argument(
   123→        "--validate-import",
   124→        dest="validate_import_file",
   125→        type=str,
   126→        metavar="FILE",
   127→        help="Validate review import payload and selected trust mode without mutating state",
   128→    )
   129→    g_core.add_argument(
   130→        "--allow-partial",
   131→        action="store_true",
   132→        help=(
   133→            "Allow partial review import when invalid findings are skipped "
   134→            "(default: fail on any skipped finding)"
   135→        ),
   136→    )
   137→    g_core.add_argument(
   138→        "--dimensions",
   139→        type=str,
   140→        default=None,
   141→        help="Comma-separated dimensions to evaluate",
   142→    )
   143→    g_core.add_argument(
   144→        "--retrospective",
   145→        action="store_true",
   146→        help=(
   147→            "Include historical review issue status/note context in the packet "
   148→            "to support root-cause vs symptom analysis during review"
   149→        ),
   150→    )
   151→    g_core.add_argument(
   152→        "--retrospective-max-issues",
   153→        type=int,
   154→        default=30,
   155→        help="Max recent historical issues to include in review context (default: 30)",
   156→    )
   157→    g_core.add_argument(
   158→        "--retrospective-max-batch-items",
   159→        type=int,
   160→        default=20,
   161→        help="Max history items included per batch focus slice (default: 20)",
   162→    )
   163→    g_core.add_argument(
   164→        "--force-review-rerun",
   165→        action="store_true",
   166→        help="Bypass the objective-plan-drained gate for review reruns",
   167→    )
   168→
   169→    # -- external review --
   170→    g_external = p_review.add_argument_group("external review")
   171→    g_external.add_argument(
   172→        "--external-start",
   173→        action="store_true",
   174→        help=(
   175→            "Start a cloud external review session (generates blind packet, "
   176→            "session id/token, and reviewer template)"
   177→        ),
   178→    )
   179→    g_external.add_argument(
   180→        "--external-submit",
   181→        action="store_true",
   182→        help=(
   183→            "Submit external reviewer JSON via a started session; "
   184→            "CLI injects canonical provenance before import"
   185→        ),
   186→    )
   187→    g_external.add_argument(
   188→        "--session-id",
   189→        type=str,
   190→        default=None,
   191→        help="External review session id for --external-submit",
   192→    )
   193→    g_external.add_argument(
   194→        "--external-runner",
   195→        choices=["claude"],
   196→        default="claude",
   197→        help="External reviewer runner for --external-start (default: claude)",
   198→    )
   199→    g_external.add_argument(
   200→        "--session-ttl-hours",
   201→        type=int,
   202→        default=24,
   203→        help="External review session expiration in hours (default: 24)",
   204→    )
   205→
   206→    # -- batch execution --
   207→    g_batch = p_review.add_argument_group("batch execution")
   208→    g_batch.add_argument(
   209→        "--run-batches",
   210→        action="store_true",
   211→        help="Run holistic investigation batches with subagents and merge/import output",
   212→    )
   213→    g_batch.add_argument(
   214→        "--runner",
   215→        choices=["codex"],
   216→        default="codex",
   217→        help="Subagent runner backend (default: codex)",
   218→    )
   219→    g_batch.add_argument(
   220→        "--parallel", action="store_true", help="Run selected batches in parallel"
   221→    )
   222→    g_batch.add_argument(
   223→        "--max-parallel-batches",
   224→        type=int,
   225→        default=3,
   226→        help=(
   227→            "Max concurrent subagent batches when --parallel is enabled "
   228→            "(default: 3)"
   229→        ),
   230→    )
   231→    g_batch.add_argument(
   232→        "--batch-timeout-seconds",
   233→        type=int,
   234→        default=20 * 60,
   235→        help="Per-batch runner timeout in seconds (default: 1200)",
   236→    )
   237→    g_batch.add_argument(
   238→        "--batch-max-retries",
   239→        type=int,
   240→        default=1,
   241→        help=(
   242→            "Retries per failed batch for transient runner/network errors "
   243→            "(default: 1)"
   244→        ),
   245→    )
   246→    g_batch.add_argument(
   247→        "--batch-retry-backoff-seconds",
   248→        type=float,
   249→        default=2.0,
   250→        help=(
   251→            "Base backoff delay for transient batch retries in seconds "
   252→            "(default: 2.0)"
   253→        ),
   254→    )
   255→    g_batch.add_argument(
   256→        "--batch-heartbeat-seconds",
   257→        type=float,
   258→        default=15.0,
   259→        help=(
   260→            "Progress heartbeat interval during parallel batch runs in seconds "
   261→            "(default: 15.0)"
   262→        ),
   263→    )
   264→    g_batch.add_argument(
   265→        "--batch-stall-warning-seconds",
   266→        type=int,
   267→        default=0,
   268→        help=(
   269→            "Emit warning when a running batch exceeds this elapsed time "
   270→            "(0 disables warnings; does not terminate the batch)"
   271→        ),
   272→    )
   273→    g_batch.add_argument(
   274→        "--batch-stall-kill-seconds",
   275→        type=int,
   276→        default=120,
   277→        help=(
   278→            "Terminate a batch when output state is unchanged and runner streams are idle "
   279→            "for this many seconds (default: 120; 0 disables kill recovery)"
   280→        ),
   281→    )
   282→    g_batch.add_argument(
   283→        "--dry-run",
   284→        action="store_true",
   285→        help="Generate packet/prompts only (skip runner/import)",
   286→    )
   287→    g_batch.add_argument(
   288→        "--run-log-file",
   289→        type=str,
   290→        default=None,
   291→        help=(
   292→            "Optional explicit path for live run log output "
   293→            "(overrides default run artifacts path)"
   294→        ),
   295→    )
   296→    g_batch.add_argument(
   297→        "--packet",
   298→        type=str,
   299→        default=None,
   300→        help="Use an existing immutable packet JSON instead of preparing a new one",
   301→    )
   302→    g_batch.add_argument(
   303→        "--only-batches",
   304→        type=str,
   305→        default=None,
   306→        help="Comma-separated 1-based batch indexes to run (e.g. 1,3,5)",
   307→    )
   308→    g_batch.add_argument(
   309→        "--scan-after-import",
   310→        action="store_true",
   311→        help="Run `scan` after successful merged import",
   312→    )
   313→
   314→    # -- trust & attestation --
   315→    g_trust = p_review.add_argument_group("trust & attestation")
   316→    g_trust.add_argument(
   317→        "--manual-override",
   318→        action="store_true",
   319→        help=(
   320→            "Allow untrusted assessment score imports. Findings always import; "
   321→            "scores require trusted blind provenance unless this override is set."
   322→        ),
   323→    )
   324→    g_trust.add_argument(
   325→        "--attested-external",
   326→        action="store_true",
   327→        help=(
   328→            "Accept external blind-run assessments as durable scores when "
   329→            "paired with --attest and valid blind packet provenance "
   330→            "(intended for cloud Claude subagent workflows)."
   331→        ),
   332→    )
   333→    g_trust.add_argument(
   334→        "--attest",
   335→        type=str,
   336→        default=None,
   337→        help=(
   338→            "Required with --manual-override or --attested-external. "
   339→            "For attested external imports include both phrases "
   340→            "'without awareness' and 'unbiased'."
   341→        ),
   342→    )
   343→
   344→    # -- post-processing --
   345→    g_post = p_review.add_argument_group("post-processing")
   346→    g_post.add_argument(
   347→        "--merge",
   348→        action="store_true",
   349→        help="Merge conceptually duplicate open review findings",
   350→    )
   351→    g_post.add_argument(
   352→        "--similarity",
   353→        type=float,
   354→        default=0.8,
   355→        help="Summary similarity threshold for merge (0-1, default: 0.8)",
   356→    )
   357→
   358→    # -- deprecated --
   359→    g_deprecated = p_review.add_argument_group("deprecated")
   360→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   361→        "--max-age",
   362→        type=int,
   363→        default=None,
   364→        action=_DeprecatedAction,
   365→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   366→    )
   367→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   368→        "--max-files",
   369→        type=int,
   370→        default=None,
   371→        action=_DeprecatedAction,
   372→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   373→    )
   374→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   375→        "--refresh",
   376→        action=_DeprecatedBoolAction,
   377→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   378→    )
   379→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   380→        "--holistic",
   381→        action=_DeprecatedBoolAction,
   382→        help="Deprecated: holistic is now the only review mode",
   383→    )
   384→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   385→        "--save-run-log",
   386→        action="store_true",
   387→        help=(
   388→            "Deprecated no-op: run logs are now always saved while running "
   389→            "(default location: run artifacts dir)"
   390→        ),
   391→    )

   394→def _add_zone_parser(sub) -> None:
   395→    p_zone = sub.add_parser("zone", help="Show/set/clear zone classifications")
   396→    p_zone.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   397→    p_zone.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   398→    zone_sub = p_zone.add_subparsers(dest="zone_action")
   399→    zone_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show zone classifications for all files")
   400→    z_set = zone_sub.add_parser("set", help="Override zone for a file")
   401→    z_set.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")
   402→    z_set.add_argument(
   403→        "zone_value",
   404→        type=str,
   405→        help="Zone (production, test, config, generated, script, vendor)",
   406→    )
   407→    z_clear = zone_sub.add_parser("clear", help="Remove zone override for a file")
   408→    z_clear.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")

   411→def _add_config_parser(sub) -> None:
   412→    p_config = sub.add_parser("config", help="Show/set/unset project configuration")
   413→    config_sub = p_config.add_subparsers(dest="config_action")

   419→    c_unset.add_argument("config_key", type=str, help="Config key name")

   422→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   423→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   424→    for lang_name in langs:
   425→        try:
   426→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   427→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   428→            fixer_names = []
   429→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   430→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   431→    fixer_help_lines.append("special: review — prepare structured review data")
   432→    return fixer_help_lines
   433→
   434→
   435→def _add_fix_parser(sub, langs: list[str]) -> None:
   436→    p_fix = sub.add_parser(
   437→        "fix",
   438→        help="Auto-fix mechanical issues",
   439→        epilog="\n".join(_fixer_help_lines(langs)),
   440→    )
   441→    p_fix.add_argument("fixer", type=str, help="What to fix")
   442→    p_fix.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   443→    p_fix.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   444→    p_fix.add_argument(
   445→        "--dry-run",
   446→        action="store_true",
   447→        help="Show what would change without modifying files",
   448→    )
   449→
   450→
   451→def _add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
   452→    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
   453→        "plan",
   454→        help="Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note",
   455→        description="""\
   456→Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
   457→Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-finding annotations.
   458→Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.""",
   459→        epilog="""\
   460→typical workflow:
   461→  desloppify scan                       # detect findings
   462→  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
   463→  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
   464→  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related findings
   465→  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
   466→  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
   467→  desloppify resolve fixed <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed
   468→
   469→subcommands:
   470→  show       Show plan metadata summary
   471→  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
   472→  reset      Reset plan to empty
   473→  move       Move findings in the queue
   474→  describe   Set augmented description
   475→  note       Set note on findings
   476→  skip       Skip findings (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
   477→  unskip     Bring skipped findings back to queue
   478→  reopen     Reopen resolved findings
   479→  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
   480→  cluster    Manage finding clusters""",
   481→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   482→    )
   483→    p_plan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   484→    p_plan.add_argument(
   485→        "--output", type=str, metavar="FILE", help="Write to file instead of stdout"
   486→    )
   487→
   488→    plan_sub = p_plan.add_subparsers(dest="plan_action")
   489→
   490→    # plan show
   491→    plan_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show plan metadata summary")
   492→
   493→    # plan queue
   494→    p_queue = plan_sub.add_parser("queue", help="Compact table of upcoming queue items")
   495→    p_queue.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=30, help="Max items (default: 30, 0=all)")
   496→    p_queue.add_argument("--tier", type=int, choices=[1, 2, 3, 4], default=None,
   497→                         help="Show only items in this tier (T1=critical .. T4=cosmetic)")
   498→    p_queue.add_argument("--cluster", type=str, default=None, metavar="NAME",
   499→                         help="Filter to a specific cluster")
   500→    p_queue.add_argument("--include-skipped", action="store_true",
   501→                         help="Include skipped items at end")
   502→
   503→    # plan reset
   504→    plan_sub.add_parser("reset", help="Reset plan to empty")
   505→
   506→    # plan move <patterns> <position> [target|N]
   507→    p_move = plan_sub.add_parser(
   508→        "move",
   509→        help="Move findings in the queue",
   510→        epilog="""\
   511→examples:
   512→  desloppify plan move security top                    # prioritize a detector
   513→  desloppify plan move "unused::src/foo.ts::*" top     # prioritize by pattern
   514→  desloppify plan move smells bottom                   # deprioritize smells
   515→  desloppify plan move unused before security          # move before another""",
   516→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   517→    )
   518→    p_move.add_argument(
   519→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   520→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   521→    )
   522→    p_move.add_argument(
   523→        "position", choices=["top", "bottom", "before", "after", "up", "down"],
   524→        help="Where to move (top, bottom, before, after, up N, down N)",
   525→    )
   526→    p_move.add_argument(
   527→        "target", nargs="?", default=None,
   528→        help="Target finding ID (for before/after) or offset (for up/down)",
   529→    )
   530→
   531→    # plan describe <patterns> "<text>"
   532→    p_describe = plan_sub.add_parser("describe", help="Set augmented description")
   533→    p_describe.add_argument(
   534→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   535→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   536→    )
   537→    p_describe.add_argument("text", type=str, help="Description text")
   538→
   539→    # plan note <patterns> "<text>"
   540→    p_note = plan_sub.add_parser("note", help="Set note on findings")
   541→    p_note.add_argument(
   542→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   543→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   544→    )
   545→    p_note.add_argument("text", type=str, help="Note text")
   546→
   547→    # plan skip <patterns> [--reason] [--review-after N] [--permanent] [--false-positive] [--note] [--attest]
   548→    p_skip = plan_sub.add_parser(
   549→        "skip",
   550→        help="Skip findings: temporary (default), --permanent (wontfix), or --false-positive",
   551→    )
   552→    p_skip.add_argument(
   553→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   554→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   555→    )
   556→    p_skip.add_argument("--reason", type=str, default=None, help="Why this is being skipped")
   557→    p_skip.add_argument(
   558→        "--review-after", type=int, default=None, metavar="N",
   559→        help="Re-surface after N scans (temporary only)",
   560→    )
   561→    p_skip.add_argument(
   562→        "--permanent", action="store_true",
   563→        help="Mark as wontfix (score-affecting, requires --note and --attest)",
   564→    )
   565→    p_skip.add_argument(
   566→        "--false-positive", action="store_true",
   567→        help="Mark as false positive (requires --attest)",
   568→    )
   569→    p_skip.add_argument("--note", type=str, default=None, help="Explanation (required for --permanent)")
   570→    p_skip.add_argument(
   571→        "--attest", type=str, default=None,
   572→        help="Attestation (required for --permanent and --false-positive)",
   573→    )
   574→
   575→    # plan unskip <patterns>
   576→    p_unskip = plan_sub.add_parser(
   577→        "unskip", help="Bring skipped findings back to queue (reopens permanent/fp in state)"
   578→    )
   579→    p_unskip.add_argument(
   580→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   581→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   582→    )
   583→
   584→    # plan reopen <patterns>
   585→    p_reopen = plan_sub.add_parser(
   586→        "reopen", help="Reopen resolved findings and move back to queue"
   587→    )
   588→    p_reopen.add_argument(
   589→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   590→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   591→    )
   592→
   593→    # plan focus <cluster> | --clear
   594→    p_focus = plan_sub.add_parser("focus", help="Set or clear active cluster focus")
   595→    p_focus.add_argument("cluster_name", nargs="?", default=None, help="Cluster name")
   596→    p_focus.add_argument("--clear", action="store_true", help="Clear focus")
   597→
   598→    # plan cluster ...
   599→    p_cluster = plan_sub.add_parser(
   600→        "cluster",
   601→        help="Manage finding clusters",
   602→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,

     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any

     8→CommandHandler = Callable[[Any], None]
     9→
    10→_COMMAND_HANDLERS: dict[str, CommandHandler] | None = None
    11→
    12→
    13→def _build_handlers() -> dict[str, CommandHandler]:
    14→    """Import all command modules and build the handler dict on first access."""
    15→    from desloppify.app.commands.config_cmd import cmd_config
    16→    from desloppify.app.commands.detect import cmd_detect
    17→    from desloppify.app.commands.dev_cmd import cmd_dev
    18→    from desloppify.app.commands.exclude_cmd import cmd_exclude

    25→    from desloppify.app.commands.review import cmd_review

    27→    from desloppify.app.commands.show import cmd_show

    29→    from desloppify.app.commands.update_skill import cmd_update_skill
    30→    from desloppify.app.commands.viz_cmd import cmd_tree, cmd_viz
    31→    from desloppify.app.commands.zone_cmd import cmd_zone
    32→

    34→        "scan": cmd_scan,
    35→        "status": cmd_status,
    36→        "show": cmd_show,
    37→        "next": cmd_next,
    38→        "resolve": cmd_resolve,
    39→        "ignore": cmd_ignore_pattern,
    40→        "exclude": cmd_exclude,
    41→        "fix": cmd_fix,
    42→        "plan": cmd_plan,
    43→        "detect": cmd_detect,
    44→        "tree": cmd_tree,
    45→        "viz": cmd_viz,
    46→        "move": cmd_move,
    47→        "zone": cmd_zone,
    48→        "review": cmd_review,
    49→        "config": cmd_config,
    50→        "dev": cmd_dev,
    51→        "langs": cmd_langs,
    52→        "update-skill": cmd_update_skill,
    53→    }
    54→
    55→
    56→def get_command_handlers() -> dict[str, CommandHandler]:
    57→    """Return cached command handler dict, building on first access."""
    58→    global _COMMAND_HANDLERS
    59→    if _COMMAND_HANDLERS is None:
    60→        _COMMAND_HANDLERS = _build_handlers()
    61→    return _COMMAND_HANDLERS
    62→
    63→
    64→__all__ = ["CommandHandler", "get_command_handlers"]

1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_findings",
    10→    "resolve_findings",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    22→
    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→
    97→def resolve_findings(
    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,
   101→    note: str | None = None,
   102→    attestation: str | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Set finding status for matches and return affected finding IDs."""
   105→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    resolved: list[str] = []
   108→    resolved_findings: list[dict] = []
   109→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   110→    for finding in match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   111→        previous_status = str(finding.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   112→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   116→        if status == "wontfix":
   117→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   118→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   119→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   120→                "captured_at": now,
   121→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   122→                "tier": finding.get("tier"),
   123→                "confidence": finding.get("confidence"),
   124→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(finding.get("detail", {})),
   125→            }
   126→        if status == "open":
   127→            finding["reopen_count"] = int(finding.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   128→            finding.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   129→            finding.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   130→            previous_note = finding.get("note")
   131→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   132→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   133→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = {
   135→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   136→                "text": attestation or note,
   137→                "attested_at": now,
   138→                "scan_verified": False,
   139→                "previous_status": previous_status,
   140→            }
   141→
   142→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   143→            "status": status,
   144→            "note": note,
   145→            "resolved_at": now,
   146→            "suppressed": False,
   147→            "suppressed_at": None,
   148→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   149→            "resolution_attestation": {
   150→                "kind": "manual",
```

1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→# Helpers
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→
    18→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    19→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    20→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    21→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    22→    if finding_id in order:
    23→        order.remove(finding_id)
    24→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _resolve_position(
    28→    order: list[str],
    29→    position: str,
    30→    target: str | None = None,
    31→    offset: int | None = None,
    32→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> int:
    34→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    35→
    36→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    37→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    38→    """
    39→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    40→
    41→    if position == "top":
    42→        return 0
    43→    if position == "bottom":
    44→        return len(order)
    45→
    46→    if position == "before" and target:
    47→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    48→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    49→                return i
    50→        return 0
    51→
    52→    if position == "after" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i + 1
    56→        return len(order)
    57→
    58→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    59→        if not finding_ids:
    60→            return 0
    61→        # Find current position of first item
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        current_idx = None
    64→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    65→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    66→            if item_id == first_id:
    67→                current_idx = i
    68→                break
    69→        if current_idx is None:
    70→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    71→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    72→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    73→
    74→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    75→        if not finding_ids:
    76→            return len(order)
    77→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    78→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    79→        current_idx = None
    80→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    81→            if item_id == first_id:
    82→                current_idx = i
    83→                break
    84→        if current_idx is None:
    85→            return len(clean_order)
    86→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    87→
    88→    return len(order)
    89→
    90→
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→# Move items
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→
    95→def move_items(
    96→    plan: PlanModel,
    97→    finding_ids: list[str],
    98→    position: str,
    99→    target: str | None = None,
   100→    offset: int | None = None,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   103→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→
   106→    # Remove from skipped if present
   107→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   108→    for fid in finding_ids:
   109→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   110→
   111→    # Remove from current position in order
   112→    for fid in finding_ids:
   113→        if fid in order:
   114→            order.remove(fid)
   115→
   116→    # Resolve insertion point
   117→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   118→
   119→    # Insert in original order
   120→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   121→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   122→
   123→    return len(finding_ids)
   124→
   125→
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→# Skip / unskip
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→
   130→def skip_items(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    finding_ids: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    kind: str = "temporary",
   135→    reason: str | None = None,
   136→    note: str | None = None,
   137→    attestation: str | None = None,
   138→    review_after: int | None = None,
   139→    scan_count: int = 0,
   140→) -> int:
   141→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    count = 0
   145→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   146→    for fid in finding_ids:
   147→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   148→        skipped[fid] = {
   149→            "finding_id": fid,
   150→            "kind": kind,
   151→            "reason": reason,
   152→            "note": note,
   153→            "attestation": attestation,
   154→            "created_at": now,
   155→            "review_after": review_after,
   156→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   157→        }
   158→        count += 1
   159→    return count
   160→
   161→
   162→def unskip_items(
   163→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   164→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   165→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   166→
   167→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   168→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   169→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   170→    """
   171→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   172→    count = 0
   173→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   174→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   175→    for fid in finding_ids:
   176→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   177→        if entry is not None:
   178→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   179→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   180→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   181→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   182→            count += 1
   183→    return count, need_reopen
   184→
   185→
   186→def resurface_stale_skips(
   187→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   188→) -> list[str]:
   189→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   190→
   191→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   192→    """
   193→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   194→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   195→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   196→    for fid in list(skipped):
   197→        entry = skipped[fid]
   198→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   199→            continue
   200→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   201→        if review_after is None:
   202→            continue
   203→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   204→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   205→            skipped.pop(fid)
   206→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   207→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   208→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   209→    return resurfaced
   210→
   211→
   212→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   213→# Describe / annotate
   214→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   215→
   216→def describe_finding(
   217→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   218→) -> None:
   219→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   220→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   221→    now = utc_now()
   222→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   223→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   224→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   225→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   226→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   227→
   228→
   229→def annotate_finding(
   230→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   231→) -> None:
   232→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   233→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   234→    now = utc_now()
   235→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   236→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   237→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   238→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   239→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   240→
   241→
   242→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   243→# Clusters
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→
   246→def create_cluster(
   247→    plan: PlanModel,
   248→    name: str,
   249→    description: str | None = None,
   250→    action: str | None = None,
   251→) -> Cluster:
   252→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   253→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   254→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   255→        raise ValueError(
   256→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   257→        )
   258→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   259→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   260→    now = utc_now()
   261→    cluster: Cluster = {
   262→        "name": name,
   263→        "description": description,
   264→        "finding_ids": [],
   265→        "created_at": now,
   266→        "updated_at": now,
   267→        "auto": False,
   268→        "cluster_key": "",
   269→        "action": action,
   270→        "user_modified": False,
   271→    }
   272→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   273→    return cluster
   274→
   275→
   276→def add_to_cluster(
   277→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   278→) -> int:
   279→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   280→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   281→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   282→    if cluster is None:
   283→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   284→
   285→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   286→    count = 0
   287→    now = utc_now()
   288→    for fid in finding_ids:
   289→        if fid not in member_ids:
   290→            member_ids.append(fid)
   291→            count += 1
   292→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   293→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   294→        if fid not in overrides:
   295→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   296→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   297→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   298→
   299→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   300→    return count
   301→
   302→
   303→def remove_from_cluster(
   304→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   305→) -> int:
   306→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   307→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   308→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   309→    if cluster is None:
   310→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   311→
   312→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   313→    now = utc_now()
   314→    count = 0
   315→    for fid in finding_ids:
   316→        if fid in member_ids:
   317→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   318→            count += 1
   319→        # Clear cluster from override
   320→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   321→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   322→            override["cluster"] = None
   323→            override["updated_at"] = now
   324→
   325→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   326→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   327→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   328→
   329→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   330→    return count
   331→
   332→
   333→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   334→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   335→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   336→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   337→    if cluster is None:
   338→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   339→
   340→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   341→    now = utc_now()
   342→    for fid in orphaned:
   343→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   344→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   345→            override["cluster"] = None
   346→            override["updated_at"] = now
   347→
   348→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   349→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   350→
   351→    return orphaned
   352→
   353→
   354→def move_cluster(
   355→    plan: PlanModel,
   356→    cluster_name: str,
   357→    position: str,
   358→    target: str | None = None,
   359→    offset: int | None = None,
   360→) -> int:
   361→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   362→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   363→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   364→    if cluster is None:
   365→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   366→
   367→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   368→    if not member_ids:
   369→        return 0
   370→
   371→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   372→
   373→
   374→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   375→# Focus
   376→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   377→
   378→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   379→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   380→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   381→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   382→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   383→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   384→
   385→
   386→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   387→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   388→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   389→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   390→
   391→
   392→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   393→# Reset
   394→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   395→
   396→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   397→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   398→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   399→    plan.clear()
   400→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan as _empty
   401→    for k, v in _empty().items():
   402→        plan[k] = v
   403→    plan["created"] = created
   404→
   405→
   406→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   407→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   408→
   409→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   410→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   411→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   412→    """
   413→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   414→    found = 0
   415→
   416→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   417→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   418→    for fid in finding_ids:
   419→        was_present = False
   420→        if fid in order:
   421→            order.remove(fid)
   422→            was_present = True
   423→        if fid in skipped:
   424→            skipped.pop(fid)
   425→            was_present = True
   426→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   427→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   428→            if fid in ids:
   429→                ids.remove(fid)
   430→                was_present = True
   431→        if was_present:
   432→            found += 1
   433→
   434→    return found
   435→
   436→
   437→__all__ = [
   438→    "add_to_cluster",
   439→    "annotate_finding",
   440→    "clear_focus",
   441→    "create_cluster",
   442→    "delete_cluster",
   443→    "describe_finding",
   444→    "move_cluster",
   445→    "move_items",
   446→    "purge_ids",
   447→    "remove_from_cluster",
   448→    "reset_plan",
   449→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   450→    "set_focus",
   451→    "skip_items",
   452→    "unskip_items",
   453→]
   454→
```

1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     9→
    10→
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    13→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output
    14→
    15→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    33→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    34→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    35→
    36→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    37→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    38→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    39→    if total_skipped:
    40→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    41→    else:
    42→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    43→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    44→    if clusters:
    45→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    46→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    47→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    48→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    49→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    50→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    51→    if described or noted:
    52→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    53→    if active:
    54→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    55→    if superseded:
    56→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    57→
    58→
    59→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    60→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    61→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    62→
    63→    plan = load_plan()
    64→    reset_plan(plan)
    65→    save_plan(plan)
    66→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    67→
    68→
    69→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    70→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    71→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    72→
    73→    if plan_action is None:
    74→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    75→        return
    76→
    77→    if plan_action == "show":
    78→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    79→        return
    80→
    81→    if plan_action == "queue":
    82→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_render import cmd_plan_queue
    83→
    84→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
    85→        return
    86→
    87→    if plan_action == "reset":
    88→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
    89→        return
    90→
    91→    if plan_action == "move":
    92→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
    93→
    94→        cmd_plan_move(args)
    95→        return
    96→
    97→    if plan_action in (
    98→        "describe", "note", "focus",
    99→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen",
   100→    ):

    47→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. [{c.type}] {c.file}", "cyan"))
    48→        print(f"     {c.summary}")
    49→        for ev in c.evidence:
    50→            print(colorize(f"       - {ev}", "dim"))

Now let me create a comprehensive summary based on my findings:

## CLI Command Overlap Audit Report

I've completed a thorough audit of the desloppify CLI for command overlaps. Here are my findings:

---

### 1. **`plan reopen` vs `resolve open`** — TRUE OVERLAP (Different Approaches)

**`resolve open [patterns]`** (top-level):
- Uses `state_mod.resolve_findings(state, pattern, "open")` 
- Only changes state-layer status back to "open"
- Works on any resolved findings matching the pattern
- Filters: `status_filter = "all"` (looks for non-open statuses to reopen)

**`plan reopen [patterns]`** (plan subcommand):
- Changes state-layer status back to "open" (via `state_mod.resolve_findings`)
- ALSO removes from plan's `skipped` dict
- ALSO ensures all reopened IDs are added back to plan's `queue_order`
- Provides more comprehensive plan synchronization

**Assessment**: These ARE redundant in functionality but serve slightly different workflows:
- `resolve open` is simpler: state-only
- `plan reopen` is more complete: state + plan synchronization

**Issue**: Users might use either one depending on context. The redundancy creates confusion. One should be deprecated or one should become a pure alias to the other.

---

### 2. **`plan skip --permanent` vs `resolve wontfix`** — COMPLEX OVERLAP

**`resolve wontfix [patterns] --note "..." --attest "..."`** (top-level):
- Sets state-layer status to "wontfix"
- Requires attestation and --note
- Removes items from living plan queue (via `purge_ids()`)
- Records `wontfix_snapshot` with scan_count, tier, confidence, detail
- Marks as score-affecting debt

**`plan skip [patterns] --permanent --note "..." --attest "..."`** (plan subcommand):
- Sets state-layer status to "wontfix" (same call: `state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, fid, "wontfix", note, attestation=attestation)`)
- Requires attestation and --note (identical validation)
- Moves to plan's `skipped` dict with `kind="permanent"`
- Records same `wontfix_snapshot` 

**Key Difference**: 
- `resolve wontfix` → removes from plan queue entirely
- `plan skip --permanent` → keeps in plan's `skipped` dict, allowing `plan unskip` to bring it back later

**Assessment**: These are INTENTIONALLY different in lifecycle semantics:
- `resolve wontfix` = permanent, gone from plan
- `plan skip --permanent` = still trackable, can be unskipped

However, both change the state layer identically, which is confusing.

---

### 3. **`plan skip --false-positive` vs `resolve false_positive`** — IDENTICAL REDUNDANCY

**`resolve false_positive [patterns] --attest "..."`** (top-level):
- Sets state-layer status to "false_positive"
- Requires attestation (no note required, unlike wontfix)
- Removes items from living plan queue (via `purge_ids()`)

**`plan skip [patterns] --false-positive --attest "..."`** (plan subcommand):
- Sets state-layer status to "false_positive" (same call)
- Requires attestation (identical validation)
- Moves to plan's `skipped` dict with `kind="false_positive"`

**Assessment**: CLEAR REDUNDANCY with the same state-level behavior but different plan-level tracking:
- Both achieve the same state-layer result
- The plan-tracking difference is the only distinction
- This creates an affordance problem: users can do the same thing two ways

---

### 4. **`plan skip --review-after N`** (Unique to plan) — NO OVERLAP

`plan skip [patterns] --review-after N`:
- Temporary skip that resurfaces after N scans
- Only available in plan, not in resolve
- Purely a plan-level feature with no state impact
- No overlap detected

---

### 5. **Other Top-Level vs Plan Commands** — NO ADDITIONAL OVERLAP

Checked these comparisons:
- `show` vs `plan show` — Different purposes (findings explorer vs plan metadata)
- `status` vs `plan` — Different purposes (score dashboard vs prioritized markdown)
- `next` (with `--cluster` flag) vs `plan queue` — Similar purposes but designed for different use cases (next-item vs queue overview)

---

### **Summary of Findings**

| Overlap | Severity | Root Cause | Recommendation |
|---------|----------|-----------|-----------------|
| `plan reopen` vs `resolve open` | **HIGH** | Both reopen findings in state; differ only in plan sync | Deprecate one (likely `resolve open`) or make it a plan-aware wrapper |
| `plan skip --permanent` vs `resolve wontfix` | **MEDIUM** | Same state-layer effect; differ in plan lifecycle | Document the distinction clearly or unify the experience |
| `plan skip --false-positive` vs `resolve false_positive` | **HIGH** | Nearly identical; only differ in plan tracking | Strong candidate for deprecation (likely `resolve false_positive`) |
| `plan skip --review-after` | **NONE** | Plan-exclusive feature | No overlap |

---

### **Key Insight**

The core issue is that the **state layer** and **plan layer** have separate resolution commands (`resolve` family vs `plan skip` family), but they:
1. Change the state layer identically for wontfix/false_positive
2. Differ only in plan-layer side effects

**Recommended Actions**:
1. **`resolve open` should be deprecated** — `plan reopen` is strictly better (state + plan sync)
2. **`resolve false_positive` should be deprecated** — `plan skip --false-positive` offers equivalent functionality with plan awareness
3. **`resolve wontfix` and `plan skip --permanent`** should have unified UX — consider making `resolve wontfix` automatically use plan-layer skipping, or document the intentional lifecycle difference more clearly

The root cause is architectural: the resolve family operates at state layer, but the living plan adds a second layer that should ideally be coupled with state changes, not separate.

   260→        epilog="""\

   263→    --note "removed import" --attest "I have actually ..."
   264→  desloppify resolve wontfix deprecated \\

   267→    --attest "I have actually ..."  """,

   289→            '--attest "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."'

   291→    )

   297→    )
   298→    p_resolve.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")

    40→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    41→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    42→    try:
    43→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    44→    except OSError as exc:

    58→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    59→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    60→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)

    63→    state_file = state_path(args)

    68→        attestation=attestation,
    69→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    70→    )
    71→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    72→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    73→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    74→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    75→        if isinstance(dim, str)

   130→    lang = resolve_lang(args)

   135→    )
   136→    if narrative.get("milestone"):

   138→

   140→    _write_resolve_query_entry(

   157→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   158→    """Add a pattern to the ignore list."""
   159→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   160→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   161→        show_attestation_requirement("Ignore", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   162→        sys.exit(1)
   163→
   164→    runtime = command_runtime(args)

   166→    state = runtime.state

   168→
   169→    config = runtime.config
   170→    config_mod.add_ignore_pattern(config, args.pattern)
   171→    config["needs_rescan"] = True
   172→    _save_config_or_exit(config)

   192→    print_score_update(state, prev)
