1→"""State-application helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query_best_effort as _write_query_best_effort
     9→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    10→
    11→from .selection import ResolveQueryContext
    12→
    13→
    14→def _try_expand_cluster(pattern: str) -> list[str] | None:
    15→    """If pattern matches a cluster name, return its finding IDs."""
    16→    try:
    17→        from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
    18→
    19→        if not has_living_plan():
    20→            return None
    21→        plan = load_plan()
    22→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(pattern)
    23→        if cluster and cluster.get("finding_ids"):
    24→            return list(cluster["finding_ids"])
    25→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    26→        pass
    27→    return None
    28→
    29→
    30→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    33→    *,
    34→    attestation: str | None,
    35→) -> list[str]:
    36→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    37→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    38→        # Check if pattern is a cluster name — expand to member IDs
    39→        cluster_ids = _try_expand_cluster(pattern)
    40→        if cluster_ids:
    41→            for fid in cluster_ids:
    42→                resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    43→                    state,
    44→                    fid,
    45→                    args.status,
    46→                    args.note,
    47→                    attestation=attestation,
    48→                )
    49→                all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    50→            continue
    51→
    52→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    53→            state,
    54→            pattern,
    55→            args.status,
    56→            args.note,
    57→            attestation=attestation,
    58→        )
    59→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    60→    return all_resolved
    61→
    62→
    63→def write_query(payload: dict) -> OutputResult:
    64→    """Backward-compatible resolve query writer seam."""
    65→    return _write_query_best_effort(
    66→        payload,
    67→        context="resolve query payload update",
    68→    )
    69→
    70→
    71→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    72→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)
    73→    write_query(
    74→        {
    75→            "command": "resolve",
    76→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    77→            "status": context.status,
    78→            "resolved": context.resolved,
    79→            "count": len(context.resolved),
    80→            "next_command": context.next_command,
    81→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    82→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    83→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    84→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    85→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,
    86→            "prev_objective_score": context.prev_objective,
    87→            "prev_strict_score": context.prev_strict,
    88→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    89→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    90→            "narrative": context.narrative,
    91→        }
    92→    )
    93→

1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    12→    verb = "Reopened" if status == "open" else "Resolved"
    13→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} finding(s) as {status}:", "green"))
    14→    for fid in all_resolved[:20]:
    15→        print(f"  {fid}")
    16→    if len(all_resolved) > 20:
    17→        print(f"  ... and {len(all_resolved) - 20} more")
    18→
    19→
    20→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    21→    state: dict,
    22→    *,
    23→    status: str,
    24→    resolved_count: int,
    25→) -> None:
    26→    if status != "wontfix" or resolved_count <= 10:
    27→        return
    28→    wontfix_count = sum(
    29→        1 for finding in state["findings"].values() if finding["status"] == "wontfix"
    30→    )
    31→    actionable = sum(
    32→        1
    33→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    34→        if finding["status"]
    35→        in ("open", "wontfix", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive")
    36→    )
    37→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    38→    print(
    39→        colorize(
    40→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} findings ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    41→            "yellow",
    42→        )
    43→    )
    44→    print(
    45→        colorize(
    46→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    47→            "dim",
    48→        )
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    53→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    54→        return ""
    55→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    56→
    57→
    58→def _print_score_movement(
    59→    *,
    60→    status: str,
    61→    prev_overall: float | None,
    62→    prev_objective: float | None,
    63→    prev_strict: float | None,
    64→    prev_verified: float | None,
    65→    state: dict,
    66→    has_review_findings: bool = False,
    67→) -> None:
    68→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    69→    if (
    70→        new.overall is None
    71→        or new.objective is None
    72→        or new.strict is None
    73→        or new.verified is None
    74→    ):
    75→        print(colorize("\n  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    overall_delta = new.overall - (prev_overall or 0)
    79→    objective_delta = new.objective - (prev_objective or 0)
    80→    strict_delta = new.strict - (prev_strict or 0)
    81→    verified_delta = new.verified - (prev_verified or 0)
    82→    print(
    83→        f"\n  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(overall_delta)}"
    84→        + colorize(
    85→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(objective_delta)}",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(strict_delta)}", "dim")
    89→        + colorize(
    90→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(verified_delta)}", "dim"
    91→        )
    92→    )
    93→    if status == "wontfix":
    94→        strict_gap = round(new.overall - new.strict, 1)
    95→        if strict_gap > 0:
    96→            print(
    97→                colorize(
    98→                    f"  Note: wontfix items still count against strict score. "
    99→                    f"Current gap: overall {new.overall:.1f} vs strict {new.strict:.1f} ({strict_gap:.1f} pts of hidden debt).",
   100→                    "yellow",
   101→                )
   102→            )
   103→    if has_review_findings and abs(overall_delta) < 0.05:
   104→        print(
   105→            colorize(
   106→                "  Scores unchanged (review findings don't affect scores directly).",
   107→                "yellow",
   108→            )
   109→        )
   110→        print(
   111→            colorize(
   112→                "  Run `desloppify review --prepare` to get updated assessment scores.",
   113→                "dim",
   114→            )
   115→        )
   116→    elif status == "fixed":
   117→        print(
   118→            colorize(
   119→                "  Verified score updates after a scan confirms the finding disappeared.",
   120→                "yellow",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→
   125→def _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   126→    *,
   127→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   128→    state: dict,
   129→    all_resolved: list[str],
   130→    prev_subjective_scores: dict[str, float],
   131→) -> None:
   132→    has_review = any(
   133→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   134→        for fid in all_resolved
   135→    )
   136→    if not has_review or not state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   137→        return
   138→
   139→    stale_dims = sorted(
   140→        dim
   141→        for dim in {
   142→            str(
   143→                state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   144→            ).strip()
   145→            for fid in all_resolved
   146→            if state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   147→        }
   148→        if dim and dim in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {})
   149→    )
   150→    if not stale_dims:
   151→        return
   152→
   153→    shown = ", ".join(stale_dims[:3])
   154→    if len(stale_dims) > 3:
   155→        shown = f"{shown}, +{len(stale_dims) - 3} more"
   156→    print(
   157→        colorize(
   158→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   159→            "yellow",
   160→        )
   161→    )
   162→    print(
   163→        colorize(
   164→            "  Next subjective step: "
   165→            + f"`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(stale_dims)}`",
   166→            "dim",
   167→        )
   168→    )
   169→
   170→
   171→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   172→    remaining = sum(
   173→        1
   174→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   175→        if finding["status"] == "open"
   176→        and not finding.get("suppressed")
   177→    )
   178→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   179→    if remaining > 0:
   180→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   181→        print(
   182→            colorize(
   183→                f"\n  {remaining} finding{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   184→                "dim",
   185→            )
   186→        )
   187→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   188→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   189→    print()
   190→    return next_command
   191→

1→"""Validation and preflight helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import copy
     7→import sys
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
    15→_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT = ("I have actually", "not gaming")
    16→
    17→
    18→def _emit_warning(message: str) -> None:
    19→    """Write resolve preflight warnings to stderr consistently."""
    20→    print(colorize(message, "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class ResolveQueryContext:
    25→    patterns: list[str]
    26→    status: str
    27→    resolved: list[str]
    28→    next_command: str
    29→    prev_overall: float | None
    30→    prev_objective: float | None
    31→    prev_strict: float | None
    32→    prev_verified: float | None
    33→    attestation: str | None
    34→    narrative: dict
    35→    state: dict
    36→
    37→
    38→def _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation: str | None) -> list[str]:
    39→    normalized = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
    40→    return [
    41→        phrase for phrase in _REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES if phrase not in normalized
    42→    ]
    43→
    44→
    45→def validate_attestation(attestation: str | None) -> bool:
    46→    return not _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    47→
    48→
    49→def show_attestation_requirement(
    50→    label: str,
    51→    attestation: str | None,
    52→    example: str,
    53→    *,
    54→    patterns: list[str] | None = None,
    55→    status: str | None = None,
    56→) -> None:
    57→    missing = _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    58→    if not attestation:
    59→        _emit_warning(f"{label} requires --attest.")
    60→    elif missing:
    61→        missing_str = ", ".join(f"'{keyword}'" for keyword in missing)
    62→        _emit_warning(
    63→            f"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."
    64→        )
    65→    _emit_warning(
    66→        f"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."
    67→    )
    68→    print(colorize(f'Example: --attest "{example}"', "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    69→    if patterns and status:
    70→        pattern_args = " ".join(f'"{p}"' for p in patterns)
    71→        print(
    72→            colorize(
    73→                f'\nCopy-paste:\n  desloppify resolve {status} {pattern_args} --attest "{example}"',
    74→                "dim",
    75→            ),
    76→            file=sys.stderr,
    77→        )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _validate_resolve_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    81→    if args.status == "wontfix" and not args.note:
    82→        _emit_warning(
    83→            "Wontfix items become technical debt. Add --note to record your reasoning for future review."
    84→        )
    85→        sys.exit(1)
    86→    if args.status == "open":
    87→        return
    88→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
    89→        show_attestation_requirement(
    90→            "Manual resolve",
    91→            attestation,
    92→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    93→            patterns=getattr(args, "patterns", None),
    94→            status=args.status,
    95→        )
    96→        sys.exit(1)
    97→
    98→
    99→def _previous_score_snapshot(state: dict) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:
   100→    """Load a score snapshot for comparison after resolve operations."""
   101→    return state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   102→
   103→
   104→def _preview_resolve_count(state: dict, patterns: list[str]) -> int:
   105→    """Count unique open findings matching the provided patterns."""
   106→    matched_ids: set[str] = set()
   107→    for pattern in patterns:
   108→        for finding in state_mod.match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter="open"):
   109→            finding_id = finding.get("id")
   110→            if finding_id:
   111→                matched_ids.add(finding_id)
   112→    return len(matched_ids)
   113→
   114→
   115→def _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   116→    state: dict,
   117→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   118→    *,
   119→    attestation: str | None,
   120→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   121→) -> float:
   122→    """Estimate strict score drop if this resolve command is applied as wontfix."""
   123→    before = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   124→    if before is None:
   125→        return 0.0
   126→
   127→    preview_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   128→    resolve_all_patterns_fn(preview_state, args, attestation=attestation)
   129→    after = state_mod.get_strict_score(preview_state)
   130→    if after is None:
   131→        return 0.0
   132→    return max(0.0, before - after)
   133→
   134→
   135→def _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   136→    state: dict,
   137→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   138→    *,
   139→    attestation: str | None,
   140→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   141→) -> None:
   142→    if args.status != "wontfix":
   143→        return
   144→
   145→    preview_count = _preview_resolve_count(state, args.patterns)
   146→    if preview_count <= 10:
   147→        return
   148→    if getattr(args, "confirm_batch_wontfix", False):
   149→        return
   150→
   151→    strict_delta = _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   152→        state,
   153→        args,
   154→        attestation=attestation,
   155→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   156→    )
   157→    _emit_warning(f"Large wontfix batch detected ({preview_count} findings).")
   158→    if strict_delta > 0:
   159→        _emit_warning(f"Estimated strict-score debt added now: {strict_delta:.1f} points.")
   160→    _emit_warning(
   161→        "Re-run with --confirm-batch-wontfix if this debt is intentional."
   162→    )
   163→    sys.exit(1)
   164→

1→"""Plan override subcommand handlers: describe, note, skip, unskip, done, reopen, focus."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.plan._resolve import resolve_ids_from_patterns
    11→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    12→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    13→    clear_focus,
    14→    load_plan,
    15→    save_plan,
    16→    set_focus,
    17→    skip_items,
    18→    unskip_items,
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→def cmd_plan_describe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    23→    """Set augmented description on findings."""
    24→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    25→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    26→        return
    27→
    28→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    29→    text: str = getattr(args, "text", "")
    30→
    31→    from desloppify.engine.plan import describe_finding
    32→
    33→    plan = load_plan()
    34→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    35→    if not finding_ids:
    36→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    for fid in finding_ids:
    40→        describe_finding(plan, fid, text or None)
    41→    save_plan(plan)
    42→    print(colorize(f"  Set description on {len(finding_ids)} finding(s).", "green"))
    43→
    44→
    45→def cmd_plan_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    46→    """Set note on findings."""
    47→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    48→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    49→        return
    50→
    51→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    52→    text: str | None = getattr(args, "text", None)
    53→
    54→    from desloppify.engine.plan import annotate_finding
    55→
    56→    plan = load_plan()
    57→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    58→    if not finding_ids:
    59→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    60→        return
    61→
    62→    for fid in finding_ids:
    63→        annotate_finding(plan, fid, text)
    64→    save_plan(plan)
    65→    print(colorize(f"  Set note on {len(finding_ids)} finding(s).", "green"))
    66→
    67→
    68→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    69→# Skip / unskip
    70→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    71→
    72→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    73→    """Skip findings — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
    74→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.selection import (
    75→        show_attestation_requirement,
    76→        validate_attestation,
    77→    )
    78→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    79→
    80→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    81→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    82→        return
    83→
    84→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    85→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
    86→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
    87→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
    88→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
    89→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
    90→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    91→
    92→    # Determine skip kind
    93→    if false_positive:
    94→        kind = "false_positive"
    95→    elif permanent:
    96→        kind = "permanent"
    97→    else:
    98→        kind = "temporary"
    99→
   100→    # Validate requirements for permanent/false_positive
   101→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   102→        if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   103→            show_attestation_requirement(
   104→                "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
   105→                attestation,
   106→                ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
   107→            )
   108→            return
   109→        if kind == "permanent" and not note:
   110→            print(
   111→                colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
   112→                file=sys.stderr,
   113→            )
   114→            return
   115→
   116→    plan = load_plan()
   117→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   118→    if not finding_ids:
   119→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   120→        return
   121→
   122→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   123→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   124→        from desloppify import state as state_mod
   125→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   126→
   127→        state_file = state_path(args)
   128→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   129→        status = "wontfix" if kind == "permanent" else "false_positive"
   130→        resolved: list[str] = []
   131→        for fid in finding_ids:
   132→            resolved.extend(
   133→                state_mod.resolve_findings(
   134→                    state_data, fid, status, note or "", attestation=attestation
   135→                )
   136→            )
   137→        if resolved:
   138→            state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   139→
   140→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   141→    count = skip_items(
   142→        plan,
   143→        finding_ids,
   144→        kind=kind,
   145→        reason=reason,
   146→        note=note,
   147→        attestation=attestation,
   148→        review_after=review_after,
   149→        scan_count=scan_count,
   150→    )
   151→    save_plan(plan)
   152→
   153→    label = {"temporary": "Skipped", "permanent": "Wontfixed", "false_positive": "Marked false positive"}
   154→    print(colorize(f"  {label[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   155→    if review_after:
   156→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   157→
   158→
   159→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   160→    """Unskip findings — bring back to queue."""
   161→    state = command_runtime(args).state
   162→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   163→        return
   164→
   165→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   166→
   167→    plan = load_plan()
   168→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   169→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   170→    if not finding_ids:
   171→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, finding_ids)
   175→    save_plan(plan)
   176→
   177→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   178→    if need_reopen:
   179→        from desloppify import state as state_mod
   180→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   181→
   182→        state_file = state_path(args)
   183→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   184→        reopened: list[str] = []
   185→        for fid in need_reopen:
   186→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, fid, "open"))
   187→        if reopened:
   188→            state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   189→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s) in state.", "dim"))
   190→
   191→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   192→
   193→
   194→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   195→# Reopen
   196→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   197→
   198→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   199→    """Reopen resolved findings from plan context."""
   200→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   201→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   202→
   203→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   204→
   205→    state_file = state_path(args)
   206→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   207→
   208→    reopened: list[str] = []
   209→    for pattern in patterns:
   210→        reopened.extend(
   211→            state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, pattern, "open")
   212→        )
   213→
   214→    if not reopened:
   215→        print(colorize("  No resolved findings matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   216→        return
   217→
   218→    state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   219→
   220→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   221→    plan = load_plan()
   222→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   223→    count = 0
   224→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   225→    for fid in reopened:
   226→        if fid in skipped:
   227→            skipped.pop(fid)
   228→            count += 1
   229→        if fid not in order:
   230→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   231→            order.add(fid)
   232→            count += 1
   233→    if count:
   234→        save_plan(plan)
   235→
   236→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s).", "green"))
   237→    if count:
   238→        print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   239→
   240→
   241→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   242→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   243→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   244→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   245→
   246→    plan = load_plan()
   247→    if clear_flag:
   248→        clear_focus(plan)
   249→        save_plan(plan)
   250→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   251→        return
   252→
   253→    if not cluster_name:
   254→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   255→        if active:
   256→            print(f"  Focused on: {active}")
   257→        else:
   258→            print("  No active focus.")
   259→        return
   260→
   261→    try:
   262→        set_focus(plan, cluster_name)
   263→    except ValueError as ex:
   264→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   265→        return
   266→    save_plan(plan)
   267→    print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   268→
   269→
   270→__all__ = [
   271→    "cmd_plan_describe",
   272→    "cmd_plan_focus",
   273→    "cmd_plan_note",
   274→    "cmd_plan_reopen",
   275→    "cmd_plan_skip",
   276→    "cmd_plan_unskip",
   277→]
   278→

1→"""Centralized score update display for all state-changing commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import format_strict_target_progress
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def print_score_update(
    12→    state: dict,
    13→    prev: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    14→    *,
    15→    config: dict | None = None,
    16→    label: str = "Scores",
    17→) -> None:
    18→    """Print score quartet with deltas and strict target progress.
    19→
    20→    Args:
    21→        state: Current state dict (scores already recomputed by save_state).
    22→        prev: ScoreSnapshot taken before the operation.
    23→        config: Project config dict (needed for target_strict_score).
    24→        label: Prefix label (default "Scores").
    25→    """
    26→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    27→    if (
    28→        new.overall is None
    29→        or new.objective is None
    30→        or new.strict is None
    31→        or new.verified is None
    32→    ):
    33→        print(colorize(f"\n  {label} unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    34→        return
    35→
    36→    overall_s, overall_c = format_delta(new.overall, prev.overall)
    37→    objective_s, objective_c = format_delta(new.objective, prev.objective)
    38→    strict_s, strict_c = format_delta(new.strict, prev.strict)
    39→    verified_s, verified_c = format_delta(new.verified, prev.verified)
    40→
    41→    print(
    42→        f"\n  {label}: "
    43→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_s}", overall_c)
    44→        + colorize(f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_s}", objective_c)
    45→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_s}", strict_c)
    46→        + colorize(f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_s}", verified_c)
    47→    )
    48→
    49→    # Show strict target progress when config is available
    50→    if config is not None:
    51→        from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
    52→        from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.core import NarrativeContext
    53→
    54→        narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    55→            state, context=NarrativeContext(command="score_update"),
    56→        )
    57→        strict_target = narrative.get("strict_target")
    58→        if strict_target:
    59→            lines, _target, _gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
    60→            for message, style in lines:
    61→                print(colorize(message, style))
    62→
    63→
    64→__all__ = ["print_score_update"]
    65→

1→<!-- desloppify-begin -->
     2→<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 2 -->
     3→---
     4→name: desloppify
     5→description: >
     6→  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
     7→  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
     8→  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
     9→  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
    10→  create a cleanup plan. Supports 28 languages.
    11→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    12→---
    13→
    14→# Desloppify
    15→
    16→## 1. Your Job
    17→
    18→Improve code quality by maximising the **strict score** honestly.
    19→
    20→**The main thing you do is run `desloppify next`** — it tells you exactly what to fix and how. Fix it, resolve it, run `next` again. Keep going.
    21→
    22→Follow the scan output's **INSTRUCTIONS FOR AGENTS** — don't substitute your own analysis.
    23→
    24→## 2. The Workflow
    25→
    26→Two loops. The **outer loop** rescans periodically to measure progress.
    27→The **inner loop** is where you spend most of your time: fixing issues one by one.
    28→
    29→### Outer loop — scan and check
    30→
    31→```bash
    32→desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
    33→desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
    34→```
    35→If not at target, work the inner loop until the queue is clear. When the queue is clear, rescan to catch cascading effects and new issues.
    36→
    37→### Inner loop — fix issues
    38→
    39→Repeat until the queue is clear:
    40→
    41→```
    42→1. desloppify next              ← tells you exactly what to fix next
    43→2. Fix the issue in code
    44→3. desloppify resolve fixed "<id>"
    45→```
    46→
    47→If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    48→
    49→**To be strategic**, use `plan` to shape what `next` gives you:
    50→```bash
    51→desloppify plan                        # see the full ordered queue
    52→desloppify plan move <pat> top         # reorder — what unblocks the most?
    53→desloppify plan cluster create <name>  # group related issues to batch-fix
    54→desloppify plan focus <cluster>        # scope next to one cluster
    55→desloppify plan defer <pat>            # push low-value items aside
    56→desloppify plan skip <pat>             # hide from next
    57→desloppify resolve fixed <pat>         # mark complete
    58→desloppify plan reopen <pat>           # reopen
    59→```
    60→
    61→### Subjective reviews
    62→
    63→The scan will prompt you when a subjective review is needed — just follow its instructions.
    64→If you need to trigger one manually:
    65→```bash
    66→desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
    67→```
    68→
    69→### Other useful commands
    70→
    71→```bash
    72→desloppify next --count 5                         # top 5 priorities
    73→desloppify next --cluster <name>                  # drill into a cluster
    74→desloppify show <pattern>                         # filter by file/detector/ID
    75→desloppify show --status open                     # all open findings
    76→desloppify resolve wontfix "<id>" --note "reason" # accept debt (lowers strict score)
    77→desloppify scan --path . --reset-subjective       # reset subjective baseline to 0
    78→```
    79→
    80→## 3. Reference
    81→
    82→### How scoring works
    83→
    84→Overall score = **40% mechanical** + **60% subjective**.
    85→
    86→- **Mechanical (40%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
    87→- **Subjective (60%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
    88→- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
    89→- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
    90→
    91→### Subjective reviews in detail
    92→
    93→- **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
    94→- **Manual path**: `desloppify review --prepare` → review per dimension → `desloppify review --import file.json`.
    95→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
    96→- Integrity: reviewers score from evidence only. Scores hitting exact targets trigger auto-reset.
    97→- Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
    98→- Stale dimensions auto-surface in `next` — just follow the queue.
    99→
   100→### Key concepts
   101→
   102→- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix → T2 quick manual → T3 judgment call → T4 major refactor.
   103→- **Auto-clusters**: related findings are auto-grouped in `next`. Drill in with `next --cluster <name>`.
   104→- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
   105→- **Wontfix cost**: widens the lenient↔strict gap. Challenge past decisions when the gap grows.
   106→- Score can temporarily drop after fixes (cascade effects are normal).
   107→
   108→## 4. Escalate Tool Issues Upstream
   109→
   110→When desloppify itself appears wrong or inconsistent:
   111→
   112→1. Capture a minimal repro (`command`, `path`, `expected`, `actual`).
   113→2. Open a GitHub issue in `peteromallet/desloppify`.
   114→3. If you can fix it safely, open a PR linked to that issue.
   115→4. If unsure whether it is tool bug vs user workflow, issue first, PR second.
   116→
   117→## Prerequisite
   118→
   119→`command -v desloppify >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "desloppify: installed" || echo "NOT INSTALLED — run: pip install --upgrade git+https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git"`
   120→
   121→<!-- desloppify-end -->
   122→
```

1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    12→    verb = "Reopened" if status == "open" else "Resolved"
    13→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} finding(s) as {status}:", "green"))
    14→    for fid in all_resolved[:20]:
    15→        print(f"  {fid}")
    16→    if len(all_resolved) > 20:
    17→        print(f"  ... and {len(all_resolved) - 20} more")
    18→
    19→
    20→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    21→    state: dict,
    22→    *,
    23→    status: str,
    24→    resolved_count: int,
    25→) -> None:
    26→    if status != "wontfix" or resolved_count <= 10:
    27→        return
    28→    wontfix_count = sum(
    29→        1 for finding in state["findings"].values() if finding["status"] == "wontfix"
    30→    )
    31→    actionable = sum(
    32→        1
    33→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    34→        if finding["status"]
    35→        in ("open", "wontfix", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive")
    36→    )
    37→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    38→    print(
    39→        colorize(
    40→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} findings ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    41→            "yellow",
    42→        )
    43→    )
    44→    print(
    45→        colorize(
    46→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    47→            "dim",
    48→        )
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    53→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    54→        return ""
    55→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    56→
    57→
    58→def _print_score_movement(
    59→    *,
    60→    status: str,
    61→    prev_overall: float | None,
    62→    prev_objective: float | None,
    63→    prev_strict: float | None,
    64→    prev_verified: float | None,
    65→    state: dict,
    66→    has_review_findings: bool = False,
    67→) -> None:
    68→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    69→    if (
    70→        new.overall is None
    71→        or new.objective is None
    72→        or new.strict is None
    73→        or new.verified is None
    74→    ):
    75→        print(colorize("\n  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    overall_delta = new.overall - (prev_overall or 0)
    79→    objective_delta = new.objective - (prev_objective or 0)
    80→    strict_delta = new.strict - (prev_strict or 0)
    81→    verified_delta = new.verified - (prev_verified or 0)
    82→    print(
    83→        f"\n  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(overall_delta)}"
    84→        + colorize(
    85→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(objective_delta)}",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(strict_delta)}", "dim")
    89→        + colorize(
    90→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(verified_delta)}", "dim"
    91→        )
    92→    )
    93→    if status == "wontfix":
    94→        strict_gap = round(new.overall - new.strict, 1)
    95→        if strict_gap > 0:
    96→            print(
    97→                colorize(
    98→                    f"  Note: wontfix items still count against strict score. "
    99→                    f"Current gap: overall {new.overall:.1f} vs strict {new.strict:.1f} ({strict_gap:.1f} pts of hidden debt).",
   100→                    "yellow",
   101→                )
   102→            )
   103→    if has_review_findings and abs(overall_delta) < 0.05:
   104→        print(
   105→            colorize(
   106→                "  Scores unchanged (review findings don't affect scores directly).",
   107→                "yellow",
   108→            )
   109→        )
   110→        print(
   111→            colorize(
   112→                "  Run `desloppify review --prepare` to get updated assessment scores.",
   113→                "dim",
   114→            )
   115→        )
   116→    elif status == "fixed":
   117→        print(
   118→            colorize(
   119→                "  Verified score updates after a scan confirms the finding disappeared.",
   120→                "yellow",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→
   125→def _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   126→    *,
   127→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   128→    state: dict,
   129→    all_resolved: list[str],
   130→    prev_subjective_scores: dict[str, float],
   131→) -> None:
   132→    has_review = any(
   133→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   134→        for fid in all_resolved
   135→    )
   136→    if not has_review or not state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   137→        return
   138→
   139→    stale_dims = sorted(
   140→        dim
   141→        for dim in {
   142→            str(
   143→                state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   144→            ).strip()
   145→            for fid in all_resolved
   146→            if state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   147→        }
   148→        if dim and dim in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {})
   149→    )
   150→    if not stale_dims:
   151→        return
   152→
   153→    shown = ", ".join(stale_dims[:3])
   154→    if len(stale_dims) > 3:
   155→        shown = f"{shown}, +{len(stale_dims) - 3} more"
   156→    print(
   157→        colorize(
   158→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   159→            "yellow",
   160→        )
   161→    )
   162→    print(
   163→        colorize(
   164→            "  Next subjective step: "
   165→            + f"`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(stale_dims)}`",
   166→            "dim",
   167→        )
   168→    )
   169→
   170→
   171→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   172→    remaining = sum(
   173→        1
   174→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   175→        if finding["status"] == "open"
   176→        and not finding.get("suppressed")
   177→    )
   178→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   179→    if remaining > 0:
   180→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   181→        print(
   182→            colorize(
   183→                f"\n  {remaining} finding{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   184→                "dim",
   185→            )
   186→        )
   187→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   188→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   189→    print()
   190→    return next_command
   191→

1→## Claude Code Overlay
     2→
     3→Use Claude subagents for subjective scoring work that should be context-isolated.
     4→
     5→### Subjective review
     6→
     7→1. **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
     8→2. **Claude cloud path**: `desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude` → use generated `claude_launch_prompt.md` + `review_result.template.json` → run printed `desloppify review --external-submit --session-id <id> --import <file>`.
     9→3. **Manual path**: split dimensions across N subagents (one message, multiple Task calls), merge outputs, then `desloppify review --import findings.json`.
    10→
    11→For the manual path:
    12→- Read `dimension_prompts` from `query.json` for dimension definitions.
    13→- Give each agent the codebase path, dimensions, and output format. Let agents decide what to read.
    14→- Each agent writes output to a separate file. Merge assessments (average overlaps) and findings.
    15→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state for correlation.
    16→
    17→### Subagent rules
    18→
    19→1. Prefer delegating review tasks to a project subagent in `.claude/agents/`.
    20→2. Set `context: fork` so prior chat context does not leak into scoring.
    21→3. For blind reviews, consume `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` instead of full `query.json`.
    22→4. Score from evidence only; do not anchor to target thresholds. When mixed, score lower.
    23→5. Return machine-readable JSON matching the format in the base skill doc. For `--external-submit`, include `session` from the generated template.
    24→6. `findings` MUST match `query.system_prompt` exactly. Use `"findings": []` when no defects found.
    25→7. Import is fail-closed: invalid findings abort unless `--allow-partial` is passed.
    26→
    27→<!-- desloppify-overlay: claude -->
    28→<!-- desloppify-end -->
    29→
```

1→"""Validation and preflight helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import copy
     7→import sys
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
    15→_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT = ("I have actually", "not gaming")
    16→
    17→
    18→def _emit_warning(message: str) -> None:
    19→    """Write resolve preflight warnings to stderr consistently."""
    20→    print(colorize(message, "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class ResolveQueryContext:
    25→    patterns: list[str]
    26→    status: str
    27→    resolved: list[str]
    28→    next_command: str
    29→    prev_overall: float | None
    30→    prev_objective: float | None
    31→    prev_strict: float | None
    32→    prev_verified: float | None
    33→    attestation: str | None
    34→    narrative: dict
    35→    state: dict
    36→
    37→
    38→def _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation: str | None) -> list[str]:
    39→    normalized = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
    40→    return [
    41→        phrase for phrase in _REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES if phrase not in normalized
    42→    ]
    43→
    44→
    45→def validate_attestation(attestation: str | None) -> bool:
    46→    return not _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    47→
    48→
    49→def show_attestation_requirement(
    50→    label: str,
    51→    attestation: str | None,
    52→    example: str,
    53→    *,
    54→    patterns: list[str] | None = None,
    55→    status: str | None = None,
    56→) -> None:
    57→    missing = _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    58→    if not attestation:
    59→        _emit_warning(f"{label} requires --attest.")
    60→    elif missing:
    61→        missing_str = ", ".join(f"'{keyword}'" for keyword in missing)
    62→        _emit_warning(
    63→            f"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."
    64→        )
    65→    _emit_warning(
    66→        f"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."
    67→    )
    68→    print(colorize(f'Example: --attest "{example}"', "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    69→    if patterns and status:
    70→        pattern_args = " ".join(f'"{p}"' for p in patterns)
    71→        print(
    72→            colorize(
    73→                f'\nCopy-paste:\n  desloppify resolve {status} {pattern_args} --attest "{example}"',
    74→                "dim",
    75→            ),
    76→            file=sys.stderr,
    77→        )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _validate_resolve_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    81→    if args.status == "wontfix" and not args.note:
    82→        _emit_warning(
    83→            "Wontfix items become technical debt. Add --note to record your reasoning for future review."
    84→        )
    85→        sys.exit(1)
    86→    if args.status == "open":
    87→        return
    88→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
    89→        show_attestation_requirement(
    90→            "Manual resolve",
    91→            attestation,
    92→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    93→            patterns=getattr(args, "patterns", None),
    94→            status=args.status,
    95→        )
    96→        sys.exit(1)
    97→
    98→
    99→def _previous_score_snapshot(state: dict) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:
   100→    """Load a score snapshot for comparison after resolve operations."""
   101→    return state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   102→
   103→
   104→def _preview_resolve_count(state: dict, patterns: list[str]) -> int:
   105→    """Count unique open findings matching the provided patterns."""
   106→    matched_ids: set[str] = set()
   107→    for pattern in patterns:
   108→        for finding in state_mod.match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter="open"):
   109→            finding_id = finding.get("id")
   110→            if finding_id:
   111→                matched_ids.add(finding_id)
   112→    return len(matched_ids)
   113→
   114→
   115→def _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   116→    state: dict,
   117→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   118→    *,
   119→    attestation: str | None,
   120→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   121→) -> float:
   122→    """Estimate strict score drop if this resolve command is applied as wontfix."""
   123→    before = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   124→    if before is None:
   125→        return 0.0
   126→
   127→    preview_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   128→    resolve_all_patterns_fn(preview_state, args, attestation=attestation)
   129→    after = state_mod.get_strict_score(preview_state)
   130→    if after is None:
   131→        return 0.0
   132→    return max(0.0, before - after)
   133→
   134→
   135→def _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   136→    state: dict,
   137→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   138→    *,
   139→    attestation: str | None,
   140→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   141→) -> None:
   142→    if args.status != "wontfix":
   143→        return
   144→
   145→    preview_count = _preview_resolve_count(state, args.patterns)
   146→    if preview_count <= 10:
   147→        return
   148→    if getattr(args, "confirm_batch_wontfix", False):
   149→        return
   150→
   151→    strict_delta = _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   152→        state,
   153→        args,
   154→        attestation=attestation,
   155→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   156→    )
   157→    _emit_warning(f"Large wontfix batch detected ({preview_count} findings).")
   158→    if strict_delta > 0:
   159→        _emit_warning(f"Estimated strict-score debt added now: {strict_delta:.1f} points.")
   160→    _emit_warning(
   161→        "Re-run with --confirm-batch-wontfix if this debt is intentional."
   162→    )
   163→    sys.exit(1)
   164→
```

    22→def cmd_plan_describe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    25→    if not require_completed_scan(state):

    72→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    73→    """Skip findings — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
    74→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.selection import (
    75→        show_attestation_requirement,
    76→        validate_attestation,
    77→    )
    78→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    79→
    80→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    81→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    82→        return
    83→
    84→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    85→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
    86→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
    87→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
    88→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
    89→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
    90→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    91→
    92→    # Determine skip kind
    93→    if false_positive:
    94→        kind = "false_positive"
    95→    elif permanent:
    96→        kind = "permanent"
    97→    else:
    98→        kind = "temporary"
    99→
   100→    # Validate requirements for permanent/false_positive
   101→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   102→        if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   103→            show_attestation_requirement(
   104→                "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
   105→                attestation,
   106→                ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
   107→            )
   108→            return
   109→        if kind == "permanent" and not note:
   110→            print(
   111→                colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
   112→                file=sys.stderr,
   113→            )
   114→            return
   115→
   116→    plan = load_plan()
   117→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   118→    if not finding_ids:
   119→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   120→        return
   121→
   122→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   123→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   124→        from desloppify import state as state_mod
   125→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   126→
   127→        state_file = state_path(args)
   128→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   129→        status = "wontfix" if kind == "permanent" else "false_positive"
   130→        resolved: list[str] = []
   131→        for fid in finding_ids:
   132→            resolved.extend(
   133→                state_mod.resolve_findings(
   134→                    state_data, fid, status, note or "", attestation=attestation
   135→                )
   136→            )
   137→        if resolved:
   138→            state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   139→
   140→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   141→    count = skip_items(
   142→        plan,
   143→        finding_ids,
   144→        kind=kind,
   145→        reason=reason,
   146→        note=note,
   147→        attestation=attestation,
   148→        review_after=review_after,
   149→        scan_count=scan_count,
   150→    )
   151→    save_plan(plan)
   152→
   153→    label = {"temporary": "Skipped", "permanent": "Wontfixed", "false_positive": "Marked false positive"}
   154→    print(colorize(f"  {label[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   155→    if review_after:
   156→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   157→
   158→
   159→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   160→    """Unskip findings — bring back to queue."""
   161→    state = command_runtime(args).state
   162→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   163→        return
   164→
   165→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   166→
   167→    plan = load_plan()
   168→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   169→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   170→    if not finding_ids:
   171→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, finding_ids)
   175→    save_plan(plan)
   176→
   177→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   178→    if need_reopen:
   179→        from desloppify import state as state_mod
   180→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   181→
   182→        state_file = state_path(args)
   183→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   184→        reopened: list[str] = []
   185→        for fid in need_reopen:
   186→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, fid, "open"))
   187→        if reopened:
   188→            state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   189→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s) in state.", "dim"))
   190→
   191→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   192→
   193→
   194→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   195→# Reopen
   196→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   197→
   198→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   199→    """Reopen resolved findings from plan context."""
   200→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   201→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   202→
   203→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   204→
   205→    state_file = state_path(args)
   206→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   207→
   208→    reopened: list[str] = []
   209→    for pattern in patterns:
   210→        reopened.extend(
   211→            state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, pattern, "open")
   212→        )
   213→
   214→    if not reopened:
   215→        print(colorize("  No resolved findings matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   216→        return
   217→
   218→    state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   219→
   220→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   221→    plan = load_plan()
   222→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   223→    count = 0
   224→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   225→    for fid in reopened:
   226→        if fid in skipped:
   227→            skipped.pop(fid)
   228→            count += 1
   229→        if fid not in order:
   230→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   231→            order.add(fid)
   232→            count += 1
   233→    if count:
   234→        save_plan(plan)
   235→
   236→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s).", "green"))
   237→    if count:
   238→        print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   239→

   241→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

   243→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)

   245→
   246→    plan = load_plan()

   250→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))

   253→    if not cluster_name:

   255→        if active:

   257→        else:

1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
     9→
    10→
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    13→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output
    14→
    15→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    33→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    34→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    35→
    36→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    37→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    38→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    39→    if total_skipped:
    40→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    41→    else:
    42→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    43→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    44→    if clusters:
    45→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    46→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    47→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    48→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    49→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    50→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    51→    if described or noted:
    52→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    53→    if active:
    54→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    55→    if superseded:
    56→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    57→
    58→
    59→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    60→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    61→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    62→
    63→    plan = load_plan()
    64→    reset_plan(plan)
    65→    save_plan(plan)
    66→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    67→
    68→
    69→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    70→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    71→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    72→
    73→    if plan_action is None:
    74→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    75→        return
    76→
    77→    if plan_action == "show":
    78→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    79→        return
    80→
    81→    if plan_action == "queue":
    82→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_render import cmd_plan_queue
    83→
    84→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
    85→        return
    86→
    87→    if plan_action == "reset":
    88→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
    89→        return
    90→
    91→    if plan_action == "move":
    92→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
    93→
    94→        cmd_plan_move(args)
    95→        return
    96→
    97→    if plan_action in (
    98→        "describe", "note", "focus",
    99→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen",
   100→    ):
   101→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
   102→            cmd_plan_describe,
   103→            cmd_plan_focus,
   104→            cmd_plan_note,
   105→            cmd_plan_reopen,
   106→            cmd_plan_skip,
   107→            cmd_plan_unskip,
   108→        )
   109→
   110→        dispatch = {
   111→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   112→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   113→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   114→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   115→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   116→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   117→        }
   118→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   119→        return
   120→
   121→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   122→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
   123→
   124→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   125→        return
   126→
   127→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   128→
   129→
   130→# Backwards-compatible alias
   131→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   132→
   133→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   134→

1→"""Query output helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.query_paths import query_file_path
    10→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    11→from desloppify.core.query import write_query as _core_write_query
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class QueryWriter:
    17→    """Command-level query artifact writer with explicit failure policy."""
    18→
    19→    query_file: Path
    20→
    21→    def write(self, data: dict) -> OutputResult:
    22→        return _core_write_query(data, query_file=self.query_file)
    23→
    24→    def write_best_effort(self, data: dict, *, context: str) -> OutputResult:
    25→        result = self.write(data)
    26→        if not result.ok:
    27→            detail = result.message or "unknown write failure"
    28→            print(
    29→                colorize(
    30→                    f"  Warning: {context} succeeded but query artifact was not written ({detail}).",
    31→                    "yellow",
    32→                ),
    33→                file=sys.stderr,
    34→            )
    35→        return result
    36→
    37→
    38→def query_writer() -> QueryWriter:
    39→    """Construct a writer bound to the active runtime query path."""
    40→    return QueryWriter(query_file=query_file_path())
    41→
    42→
    43→def write_query(data: dict) -> OutputResult:
    44→    """Write structured query output using default best-effort policy."""
    45→    return write_query_best_effort(data, context="query payload update")
    46→
    47→
    48→def write_query_best_effort(data: dict, *, context: str) -> OutputResult:
    49→    """Write query payload and emit a contextual warning on failure."""
    50→    return query_writer().write_best_effort(data, context=context)
    51→
    52→
    53→__all__ = [
    54→    "QueryWriter",
    55→    "query_file_path",
    56→    "query_writer",
    57→    "write_query",
    58→    "write_query_best_effort",
    59→]
    60→

1→"""Resolve findings or apply ignore-pattern suppressions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    14→from desloppify.core import config as config_mod
    15→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_error
    16→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    17→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
    18→from desloppify.state import coerce_assessment_score
    19→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    20→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness
    21→
    22→from .apply import _resolve_all_patterns, _write_resolve_query_entry
    23→from .render import (
    24→    _print_next_command,
    25→    _print_resolve_summary,
    26→    _print_score_movement,
    27→    _print_subjective_reset_hint,
    28→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning,
    29→)
    30→from .selection import (
    31→    ResolveQueryContext,
    32→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation,
    33→    _previous_score_snapshot,
    34→    show_attestation_requirement,
    35→    validate_attestation,
    36→    _validate_resolve_inputs,
    37→)
    38→
    39→
    40→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    41→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    42→    try:
    43→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    44→    except OSError as exc:
    45→        print_error(f"could not save state: {exc}")
    46→        sys.exit(1)
    47→
    48→
    49→def _save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    50→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    51→    try:
    52→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    53→    except OSError as exc:
    54→        print_error(f"could not save config: {exc}")
    55→        sys.exit(1)
    56→
    57→
    58→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    59→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    60→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    61→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    62→
    63→    state_file = state_path(args)
    64→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    65→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    66→        state,
    67→        args,
    68→        attestation=attestation,
    69→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    70→    )
    71→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    72→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    73→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    74→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    75→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
    79→    if not all_resolved:
    80→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    81→        print(
    82→            colorize(
    83→                f"No {status_label} findings matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}",
    84→                "yellow",
    85→            )
    86→        )
    87→        return
    88→
    89→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    90→
    91→    # Remove resolved items from the living plan queue (best-effort)
    92→    try:
    93→        from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan, purge_ids, save_plan
    94→
    95→        if has_living_plan():
    96→            plan = load_plan()
    97→            purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    98→            if purged:
    99→                save_plan(plan)
   100→                print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
   101→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
   102→        print(colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
   103→
   104→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   105→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   106→        state,
   107→        status=args.status,
   108→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   109→    )
   110→    has_review_findings = any(
   111→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   112→        for fid in all_resolved
   113→    )
   114→    _print_score_movement(
   115→        status=args.status,
   116→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   117→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   118→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   119→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   120→        state=state,
   121→        has_review_findings=has_review_findings,
   122→    )
   123→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   124→        args=args,
   125→        state=state,
   126→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   127→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   128→    )
   129→
   130→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   131→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   132→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   133→        state,
   134→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   135→    )
   136→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   137→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   138→
   139→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   140→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   141→        ResolveQueryContext(
   142→            patterns=args.patterns,
   143→            status=args.status,
   144→            resolved=all_resolved,
   145→            next_command=next_command,
   146→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   147→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   148→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   149→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   150→            attestation=attestation,
   151→            narrative=narrative,
   152→            state=state,
   153→        )
   154→    )
   155→
   156→
   157→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   158→    """Add a pattern to the ignore list."""
   159→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   160→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   161→        show_attestation_requirement("Ignore", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   162→        sys.exit(1)
   163→
   164→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   165→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   166→    state = runtime.state
   167→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   168→
   169→    config = runtime.config
   170→    config_mod.add_ignore_pattern(config, args.pattern)
   171→    config["needs_rescan"] = True
   172→    _save_config_or_exit(config)
   173→
   174→    removed = state_mod.remove_ignored_findings(state, args.pattern)
   175→    state.setdefault("attestation_log", []).append(
   176→        {
   177→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
   178→            "command": "ignore",
   179→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   180→            "attestation": attestation,
   181→            "affected": removed,
   182→        }
   183→    )
   184→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   185→
   186→    print(colorize(f"Added ignore pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
   187→    if removed:
   188→        print(f"  Removed {removed} matching findings from state.")
   189→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   190→    if config_warning:
   191→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   192→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   193→
   194→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   195→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   196→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   197→        state,
   198→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="ignore"),
   199→    )
   200→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   201→    write_query(
   202→        {
   203→            "command": "ignore",
   204→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   205→            "removed": removed,
   206→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
   207→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
   208→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
   209→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   210→            "attestation": attestation,
   211→            "narrative": narrative,
   212→        }
   213→    )
   214→

1→"""State-application helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query_best_effort as _write_query_best_effort
     9→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    10→
    11→from .selection import ResolveQueryContext
    12→
    13→
    14→def _try_expand_cluster(pattern: str) -> list[str] | None:
    15→    """If pattern matches a cluster name, return its finding IDs."""
    16→    try:
    17→        from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
    18→
    19→        if not has_living_plan():
    20→            return None
    21→        plan = load_plan()
    22→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(pattern)
    23→        if cluster and cluster.get("finding_ids"):
    24→            return list(cluster["finding_ids"])
    25→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    26→        pass
    27→    return None
    28→
    29→
    30→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    33→    *,
    34→    attestation: str | None,
    35→) -> list[str]:
    36→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    37→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    38→        # Check if pattern is a cluster name — expand to member IDs
    39→        cluster_ids = _try_expand_cluster(pattern)
    40→        if cluster_ids:
    41→            for fid in cluster_ids:
    42→                resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    43→                    state,
    44→                    fid,
    45→                    args.status,
    46→                    args.note,
    47→                    attestation=attestation,
    48→                )
    49→                all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    50→            continue
    51→
    52→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    53→            state,
    54→            pattern,
    55→            args.status,
    56→            args.note,
    57→            attestation=attestation,
    58→        )
    59→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    60→    return all_resolved
    61→
    62→
    63→def write_query(payload: dict) -> OutputResult:
    64→    """Backward-compatible resolve query writer seam."""
    65→    return _write_query_best_effort(
    66→        payload,
    67→        context="resolve query payload update",
    68→    )
    69→
    70→
    71→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    72→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)
    73→    write_query(
    74→        {
    75→            "command": "resolve",
    76→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    77→            "status": context.status,
    78→            "resolved": context.resolved,
    79→            "count": len(context.resolved),
    80→            "next_command": context.next_command,
    81→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    82→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    83→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    84→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    85→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,
    86→            "prev_objective_score": context.prev_objective,
    87→            "prev_strict_score": context.prev_strict,
    88→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    89→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    90→            "narrative": context.narrative,
    91→        }
    92→    )
    93→

    71→class _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    72→    """Argparse parser variant that disables long-option abbreviation."""
    73→
    74→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    75→        kwargs.setdefault("allow_abbrev", False)
    76→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
